## Neon Night Market Food Photography Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, fütüristik yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Hyperrealistic {argument name="dish" default="DISH"} shot against pure black, dramatic {argument name="color 1" default="warm"} and {argument name="color 2" default="electric"} neon rim lighting. {argument name="vessel" default="VESSEL"} center, hero ingredients fully photorealistic and craveable, every texture glistening, sauce drips and cheese pulls catching neon light like liquid glass, steam wisps glowing in colored light. Supporting ingredients floating dramatically around, each lit by neon glow. Labels in neon-glow condensed uppercase with 90° elbow joints: "[INGREDIENT preparation, texture, origin]" and repeat for 6 ingredients. Each label a different neon hue. Dish name in bold neon sign typography at top: "{argument name="dish name" default="[DISH NAME]"}". Subtitle: "[TAGLINE]". Pure black background, 4K tack sharp, warm food tones glowing against darkness, craveable.
```

## Formulaic Prompt Structure for High Fashion

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Render_Target =  ( [MODEL] * Structural_High_Fashion_Attire ) +  ( [SETTING] * AI_Calculated_Environment_Lighting ) +  ( [OCCASION/VIBE] * Model_Expression_And_Pose ) +  ( Hasselblad_H6D_100c * Sharp_Focus * 1.5 ) + ( Geometric_Shadows_OR_Studio_Strobes * 1.2 ) -  ( Clutter / 2 ) -  ( Plastic_Skin / 2 ) -  ( Amateur_Photography / 2 )  # System Note: The AI must adapt the "AI_Calculated_Environment_Lighting" dynamically to whatever [SETTING] is inputted.
```

## Slow-Motion Cosmetic Ad Animation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image as the exact visual reference.
Keep the product centered.
Do not change the tube position, lighting tone, flower placement, or color palette.
Maintain the deep blue monochromatic aesthetic.
No additional lights.
No color grading changes.
Camera:
Locked camera.
No zoom.
No rotation.
No angle change.
Static premium composition.
Action:
Animate the surrounding voluminous blue swirling clouds in ultra slow motion.
The cloud-like forms (soft fabric/ink-style swirls) gently move and curl around the product.
Movement should feel fluid, heavy, and luxurious.
Soft rolling motion from bottom to top.
Subtle inward spiral motion around the tube.
No aggressive motion.
No fast movement.
No deformation of the product.
Flowers:
Remain mostly stable.
Very subtle natural micro movement as if lightly affected by airflow.
Petals should not detach.
No new petals added.
Physics:
Smooth, dense, smoke-like simulation.
Velvety texture.
Soft shadow transitions within the folds.
Natural depth and layering.

Mood:
Elegant.
Premium.
Artistic beauty campaign.
High-end cosmetic advertisement.

Sound (optional if supported):
Soft ambient atmospheric tone.
Very subtle airy texture.
No voiceover.
End Frame:
Clouds continue slowly flowing around the product.
Product remains sharp and perfectly centered.
Ultra realistic.
Luxury slow-motion finish.
```

## Dreamy Luxury Perfume Photography in Nature Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic dreamy product photography in a serene natural paradise environment. A luxurious transparent glass perfume bottle with soft blush-toned fragrance liquid sits centered on a smooth wet stone, slightly elevated above shallow flowing water. The bottle features a minimalist matte gold cap and an elegant cream label with refined serif branding and subtle embossed detailing. Tiny water droplets rest on the glass surface, catching the light and enhancing the premium gemini feel.
In the background, a majestic waterfall cascades down moss-covered rocks, softly blurred to create depth while maintaining environmental clarity. Lush green foliage surrounds the scene, with delicate mist floating in the air and sunlight filtering through trees, creating gentle volumetric light rays.
Lighting is soft natural daylight mixed with subtle cinematic glow, producing glossy highlights on the glass and realistic reflections on the wet stone. Clean symmetrical composition with the bottle perfectly sharp in focus, shallow depth of field, highly detailed textures on water, stone, glass, and foliage. Aesthetic blends luxury fragrance advertising with ethereal nature fantasy, color palette dominated by fresh greens, soft neutrals, and crystal-clear water tones. Photorealistic 8K quality, hyper-detailed textures, premium editorial finish, high dynamic range, professional 85mm lens feel.
```

## Luxury Resort Western Editorial Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the face in the photo (identity reference). Create an image. Ultra-detailed, ultra-realistic, high-fashion editorial.
Concept
A warm, minimalist luxury-home interior editorial with a confident “resort meets western” vibe. The subject is posed in a relaxed, seductive fashion look—clean composition, natural warmth, and polished magazine styling.
Composition & Pose
Full-body, vertical frame (9:16).
Subject sits sideways on a thick concrete kitchen island/countertop.
One leg bent up on the counter, the other leg extended downward.
Torso angled slightly toward camera, shoulders relaxed.
Head tilted slightly, resting on one hand (elbow on raised knee).
Expression: calm, sultry, bored-luxury gaze, eyes looking directly at camera.
Hands and fingers posed softly (no tension), elegant wrist angle.
Wardrobe & Styling
Outfit: white triangle two-piece with thin straps and tie-side bottoms (minimal, sleek, not patterned).
Center detail: small gold ring/connector at the bikini top center.
Footwear: tall knee-high to mid-knee brown leather boots, equestrian/Western style.
Multiple straps + metal buckles, slightly structured shafts, low heel.
Jewelry: bold gold statement necklace (chunky round elements), gold bracelet/bangle, subtle gold accents (no silver).
Hat: A high-fashion Western cowboy hat in deep espresso or rich chocolate felt, perfectly matching the tone of the leather boots. Structured crown with a sharp center crease and sculpted side dents. Wide, dramatic curved brim with a subtle upward lift at the sides for a powerful silhouette. Smooth matte felt texture (luxury quality, not rustic). Finished with a sleek dark leather hatband that subtly echoes the boot straps — minimal metallic detailing in brushed gold to match the jewelry. The hat sits slightly tilted forward, casting a soft shadow over the eyes for a bold, editorial, confident mood.
Hair
Long, sleek, dark brown/espresso hair.
Smooth, glossy finish with a soft side part.
Hair falls over shoulders naturally, minimal flyaways, refined editorial polish.
Korean Makeup (matching this vibe) + Long Lashes
Skin: semi-matte “clean Korean glam” base, smooth but realistic skin texture (no plastic), soft bronzed warmth.
Brows: straight-to-soft-arched Korean brow, natural fullness, tidy edges.
Eyes:
Soft brown gradient shadow with subtle contour at outer corners (no harsh smoky).
Thin, sharp brown eyeliner with a tiny lifted wing (fox-eye hint but still Korean).
Very long, wispy lashes (doll-like length, separated, curled; emphasize outer corners).
Lower lash: light definition + subtle aegyo-sal highlight (natural, not cartoon).
Cheeks: muted peach-beige blush placed high (sun-sun-kissed but refined).
Lips: glossy gradient lip in warm rosy nude / peachy rose (hydrated, slightly overlined softly).
Highlight: controlled glow on cheekbones and nose bridge (no glitter, no oily shine).
Environment (Luxury Minimalist Kitchen
```

## Hyperrealistic Nature-Reclaiming-Tech Poster

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a stylish hyperrealistic poster featuring a vibrant, scene with soft lighting and saturated colors. The main subject is a [{argument name="device" default="I phone air"} ] adorned with the [{argument name="logo" default="iphone"}] logo and name, positioned centrally in a blooming alpine meadow. Lush vines and delicate morning glory flowers intricately wrap around the object, as if nature is reclaiming it. The background showcases snow-capped mountains under a cinematic sky. Use a dynamic angle with a shallow depth of field, focusing sharply on the object while blending soft mist and atmospheric haze. The lighting should be sunny contrasting against cool mountain shadows. The color palette combines rich greens, deep blues, and warm tones. Render the scene in a photorealistic DSLR style, with polished details and a cinematic vignette for a high-end editorial look.
```

## Luxury Skincare Commercial Video Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik ürün fotoğrafı üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Luxury skincare commercial, ultra-premium aesthetic. Use the provided product image as the exact reference for bottle shape, label design, liquid tone, reflections, and overall styling.

The bottle stands perfectly upright at the center of the frame, surrounded by a glossy liquid surface with smooth, elegant ripples. Soft fluid splashes form a graceful crown around the base, catching light like crystal. Background is a dreamy pastel aqua-to-soft lavender gradient with a silky, creamy atmosphere and subtle mist particles floating in the air.

Camera motion:
Slow, continuous 360-degree cinematic orbit around the product. Movement is smooth, controlled, and hypnotic. Constant speed throughout. No shaking, no zooming, no speed changes. The product remains perfectly centered and stable at all times.

Framing:
Mid close-up beauty shot. Bottle remains vertical and motionless, hero focus.

Lighting:
High-end studio lighting, soft diffused glow with gentle highlights and glossy reflections. Subtle rim light to outline the bottle. Liquid and glass look luminous, hydrated, and silky.

Mood & style:
Sensual, elegant, and premium beauty campaign. Smooth motion, glossy textures, soft reflections, cinematic depth of field, slow-motion feel, luxury skincare advertisement, 4K photorealistic, polished commercial look.
```

## Macro Detail Portrait with Glasses and Skin Texture Prompt

A highly detailed, hyper-realistic close-up portrait of a young woman. She has natural, un-retouched skin texture with visible pores, slight freckles, and minor blemishes. She has striking hazel eyes and her dark hair is pulled back tightly. She is wearing oversized, round tortoiseshell glasses with distinct, soft rectangular light reflections on the lenses. She is dressed in a thick, textured, taupe-grey waffle-knit crewneck sweater. The lighting is warm and dramatic studio lighting, casting deep, defined shadows on one side of her face. The background is a solid, dark rust-red/terracotta color. Shot on an 85mm lens, macro detail, cinematic lighting, 8k resolution, photorealistic." If you want to recreate the specific angles, you can add these modifiers to the beginning of the prompt:
•For the first image: "A low-angle, front-facing portrait of a young woman looking slightly upwards..."
•For the second image: "An extreme macro close-up of a woman's eye and glasses frame, focusing heavily on skin texture..."
•For the third image: "A portrait of a young woman with her head slightly tilted, offering a gentle, subtle smile to the camera..."
```

## Black and White Studio Portrait with Identity Preservation

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a photo strictly based on the source photo of the girl, 1-to-1 match.
Exact resemblance, without altering facial features or natural asymmetry.
Do not change the shape of the eyes, lips, nose, cheekbones, or face oval.
Natural skin without retouching or a plastic look.
Makeup: long brown eyelashes, smudged brown winged eyeliner, neat matte lip tone in a cool nude palette.
Hair is blonde, styled smoothly with soft volume, exactly as in the source photo, without changing length or shape.
Physique and Proportions
Height 178 cm. Thin, ascetic physique, long limbs. Proportions are strictly natural, unaltered.
Scene and Action
Portrait in a dark studio. The girl is partially hidden behind a vertical black panel/wall, with only half of her face visible. One hand gently touches the edge of the panel, fingers gracefully extended. Gaze directed straight at the camera — calm, cold, confident. Facial expression is reserved, mysterious.
Clothing
Black turtleneck dress made of thick fabric with a slight velvet texture. Long sleeves.
Nails are painted with dark glossy polish.
Minimalist stud earrings.
Environment
Deep dark background without details.
Minimalism.
The entire composition is built on the contrast of light and shadow.
Lighting
Hard directional studio lighting from the side (Rembrandt lighting). Half of the face is illuminated, the other half fades into a soft, deep shadow. Clear lighting pattern highlighting the cheekbones and lip line.
No overexposure.
Camera
Full-frame camera, 85mm lens. Shallow depth of field. No wide-angle distortion.
Focus on the visible eye and lip line.
Quality
Black and white photography with deep contrasts. Maximum photorealism. High detailing of skin and fabric.
Negative Prompt
Any changes in appearance, altering facial features and asymmetry, skin retouching, CGI, anime style, soft glamour lighting, wide-angle distortion, extra limbs, deformed fingers, overexposure.
```

## Hyper-Defined Mirror Selfie Prompt (Billie Eilish Identity Lock)

> Fotogerçekçi mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(9:16). Raw iPhone 17 Pro, 35mm, f/5.6 total depth, sharp focus, authentic digital grain.

[Subject & Identity Locking]
* Identity Lock: Absolute inheritance of core facial geometry from Image A + Glow-Up Protocol. 
* Bio-Fidelity: Clear porcelain epidermal canvas, zero acne/blemishes/dark circles, undereye brightness, micro-pores. 
* Facial Refinement: Architectural bone structure—20% increased definition to the jawline and cheekbone coordinates for a hyper-slimmed, contoured facial silhouette.
* Hair Architecture: High-gloss salon-finish long brunette hair with high-definition strand separation and silk-weave texture.

[Outfit & Physicality]
* Technical Description: Technical off-the-white floral-lace architectural gown, long sleeves with 5mm eyelash-lace scalloped cuffs, square neckline.
* Textile Architecture: High-tensile corset-style bodice with horizontal bandage-wrap structural seaming; layered lace-on-nude-base textile physics.
* Anatomical Volume: Hyper-defined S-curve silhouette, 20% over-described emphasis on anatomical bust volume, waist-to-hip ratio, and lateral hip geometry.

[Pose & Interaction]
* Geometry: Mirror-selfie geometry, right hand holding a high-fidelity pink smartphone simulation; head tilted slightly downward; face partially obscured by device.
* Articulation: Left hand resting flat against the superior chest margin with realistic finger-joint articulation and light skin-on-skin compression.

[Environment & Lighting]
* Atmosphere: Soft indoor ambient illumination with a neutral white balance and realistic ray-traced reflections.
* Shadow Ray-Tracing: Natural ambient occlusion within the intricate lace textures and sharp shadows cast by the subject onto the background masonry.
* Background Integrity: Pixel-perfect inventory of an interior staircase setting: light multi-tonal beige brick masonry wall, horizontal dark wood banister, and vertical white-painted wooden balusters.

Subject: Billie Eilish
```

Ultra-realistic 8K cinematic portrait captured during golden hour. Warm sunset light naturally illuminates the subject’s face from a low-angle sun position, creating soft highlights and gentle shadow falloff.

Hair is voluminous and well-styled with natural texture and movement. He wears premium designer sunglasses with a refined, modern silhouette and subtle reflective lenses catching the golden light.

Outfit: matte white Adidas padded puffer jacket with clean stitching and subtle fabric texture, high-quality finish, minimal branding visibility. A thin, subtle gold chain rests naturally around the neck, adding a refined luxury detail.

Background: softly blurred urban city buildings during sunset, warm orange and amber tones, natural bokeh, cinematic depth separation. The environment feels elevated and modern but not exaggerated.

Lighting: natural golden hour sunlight only, no artificial glow, no studio lighting. Soft rim light along hair and jacket edges. Realistic skin tones with visible natural texture, no smoothing, no beautification.

Camera: full-frame mirrorless, 85mm lens, f/1.8, ISO 100, 1/500s. Shallow depth of field, sharp focus on face and upper torso.

Style: modern luxury editorial photography, natural realism, high-end fashion aesthetic.

CRITICAL: Use 100% of the facial features from the uploaded reference image. Do not alter facial structure, proportions, age, skin texture, identity, or expression baseline. Absolute face preservation required.

## Luxury Lifestyle Billiards Scene with High-End Cars

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A luxurious modern indoor lounge with warm ambient lighting and minimalist architecture. A sleek black pool table sits in the foreground. A stylish man (same face as reference image) wearing a black NY cap, over-ear headphones, black leather jacket, white t-shirt, and a backpack is positioned in a professional billiards stance.
He bends low with a straight spine and balanced posture, left hand firmly bridged on the table forming a stable V-shape, right hand guiding the cue smoothly. ## High-Fashion Press Scrum Photograph Prompt

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform the subject into a high-fashion press scrum photograph, surrounded tightly by cameras, microphones, and boom mics from all directions. Preserve the subject's face, features, and overall identity clearly recognizable. The subject is centered in the frame, partially obscured by overlapping microphones and camera lenses pointing inward, creating a sense of pressure and attention. Expression is calm, controlled, and unreadable - confident but distant. Styling is minimal and fashion-forward: dark clothing, sharp silhouette, sunglasses or statement eyewear optional. The image is shot like a real editorial photograph - direct flash, harsh highlights, deep shadows, and realistic textures on equipment. Colors are cool and neutral, dominated by black, grey, and metallic tones. The composition feels claustrophobic and symmetrical, with the subject visually trapped by media attention. Style: paparazzi press swarm, fashion editorial photography, cinematic realism, high-contrast flash, modern celebrity moment.
```

## Surreal Zero-Gravity Explosion Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, retro moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A photographic surrealist shot of The Protagonist, appearing as a detached observer, standing amidst a chaotic zero-gravity explosion inside a cluttered vintage poolside cabana. The subject wears a high-fashion, textured swimwear piece. The environment is densely packed with hundreds of summer artifacts frozen in mid-air: melting ice cream cones, retro sunglasses, striped towels, splashes of chlorinated water, and inflatable beach balls, all suspended as if time has stopped. The lighting is a mix of harsh, directional sunlight casting sharp, long shadows across the floating debris, combined with a cool-toned, ghostly fill light. The composition utilizes a severe Vertigo Dutch Angle (25-degree tilt) to induce disorientation. Shot on a Hasselblad H6D-100c with a wide 35mm lens, high aperture (f/11) to keep the dense clutter in focus, processed with the grain and color shift of Kodak Ektachrome E100 film. Hyper-realistic textures, practical effect aesthetic, no CGI feel.
```

## Neon Night Market Food Photography

> Sinematik, fütüristik yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt Studio: Neon Night Market, Food, with Nano Banana Pro, in Firefly  

Hyperreal {argument name="dish" default="[DISH]"} floating against a pitch black background, dramatically lit by neon {argument name="color" default="[COLOR]"} underlighting and rim lighting. Ingredients separated and suspended in mid-air in a loose diagonal cascade, not perfectly stacked, but dynamically scattered as if caught mid-explosion. Steam and sauce splatter glow in the neon light. Sauce drips catch the light like liquid glass. Each floating ingredient has a glowing neon label tag, like a price tag on a wire, in uppercase bold font, backlit. Dish name "{argument name="dish name" default="[DISH NAME]"}" in large neon sign typography at top, flickering glow effect. Subtitle: "[TAGLINE]". Mood: 3am street food energy, cinematic, electric, craveable. Style ref: Blade Runner 2049 meets Tokyo night market meets hyperreal CGI food ad. 4K, ultra sharp, chromatic aberration on edges.
```

## Cinematic Office Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a vertical cinematic photorealistic portrait with subtle retro film grading and a moody, voyeuristic office atmosphere.

A young adult woman stands in a narrow office copy room, leaning back against a large multifunction photocopier positioned slightly to the right. She crosses her legs at the ankles and folds her arms across her chest, looking directly at the camera with a calm, slightly distant expression. She has straight, shoulder-length black hair with full bangs. She wears a fitted white button-up blouse, a striped ribbon bow tie, a high-waisted dark mini skirt, and sheer black pantyhose. Fabrics appear realistic: matte cotton blouse, structured skirt, translucent nylon stockings.

The environment includes light-colored walls, a wall poster with Japanese text, and partially visible shelving. The copier shows realistic plastic and metal textures. The space feels tight and enclosed.

Frame the shot at eye level in a medium full-body composition. Shoot through a narrow foreground gap, with dark blurred vertical shapes on both sides forming a natural vignette. Keep the subject sharply focused, especially her face and upper torso, with shallow depth of field and softly blurred background.

Use soft diffused indoor overhead lighting with slight frontal illumination, moderate contrast, and gentle shadows. Apply cool greenish-teal shadows with warm skin tones, slightly desaturated colors (muted teal, beige, off-white, charcoal, deep black). Add subtle fine film grain and balanced dynamic range to maintain an intimate, slightly nostalgic cinematic realism.
```

## Flash Editorial Kiss Portrait (Image-to-Image)

{
  "generation_request": {

      "preserve_facial_features": true,
      "preserve_pose": true,
      "preserve_hand_structure": true,
      "notes": "Use the uploaded image as structural anchor. Maintain facial proportions, hairstyle (long dark hair with volume), and the blowing-kiss gesture toward camera (hand lifted near lips, eyes closed, lips puckered). Keep the strapless red satin dress silhouette and gold jewelry. Maintain the clean white wall background and strong direct-flash aesthetic with a crisp shadow behind her. Improve realism only: natural skin texture, accurate satin highlights, correct anatomy. No text, no logos, no watermark."
    },
    "output_settings": {
      "aspect_ratio": "4:5",
      "orientation": "portrait",
      "resolution_target": "ultra_high_res",
      "num_images": 1,
      "sharpness": "editorial_crisp",
      "grain": "subtle",
      "color_grade": "clean_flash_contrast"
    },
    "scene": {
      "concept": "playful high-fashion party portrait with direct flash",
      "environment": "minimal matte white wall studio",
      "composition": "tight 3/4 portrait; subject filling frame; maintain original crop",
      "lighting": {
        "style": "on-camera direct flash",
        "quality": "hard light",
        "shadow": "distinct hard shadow on wall",
        "notes": "avoid soft studio diffusion; keep authentic flash falloff"
      }
    },
    "camera": {
      "shot_type": "tight portrait",
      "camera_angle": "eye_level",
      "lens": "50mm prime",
      "aperture": "f/4",
      "shutter": "1/160",
      "iso": "200",
      "white_balance": "flash_daylight",
      "focus": "face and hand"
    },
    "subject": {
      "person": "adult woman",
      "expression": "playful, eyes closed, lips puckered mid-kiss",
      "makeup": "clean glam, defined lashes, soft blush, satin nude-rose lips",
      "hair": "dark, full volume, natural shine",
      "wardrobe": {
        "dress": "strapless vivid red satin dress with structured bodice",
        "jewelry": "gold hoops, delicate necklace, minimal bracelets and rings"
      }
    },
    "style_rules": {
      "aesthetic": "luxury editorial flash snapshot",
      "texture_policy": "preserve pores, realistic fabric sheen, natural hair strands",
      "avoid": [
        "beauty filter smoothing",
        "plastic skin",
        "AI symmetry artifacts",
        "extra fingers",
        "background gradients",
        "text overlays",
        "watermarks"
      ]
    },
    "negative_prompt": "bad anatomy, extra fingers, fused fingers, deformed hands, warped face, asymmetrical eye"
  }
}

Subject is leaning/sitting in a relaxed pose with one hand slightly raised near the face (fingers softly bent), the other hand resting lower near the waist. Add a strong multi-exposure / motion-echo effect: 2–3 translucent offset duplicates trailing to the right, like a long-exposure movement smear. Keep the main face sharp and dominant, with the echoes softer and semi-transparent. Lux editorial retouching, crisp fabric texture, subtle skin texture, cinematic contrast. Clean image: no text, no logos, no watermark."

      },
      "camera": {

      }

## Ultra-realistic cinematic 9-photo collage of a gym girl's fitness journey

Ultra-realistic cinematic 9-photo collage collection telling a complete fitness journey story of a young athletic woman traveling from her home to the gym and returning back home, each frame showing a different scene and progression, strong visual storytelling, consistent character identity across all images (same face, same hairstyle, same gym outfit), natural body proportions, photorealistic skin texture, lifestyle editorial photography, no CGI, no illustration, ultra-detailed, 8K resolution.
Frame 1: Inside her bedroom, she prepares for the gym, adjusting her workout clothes in front of a mirror, soft morning sunlight entering through the window, calm focused mood.
Frame 2: At the home entrance, she picks up her gym bag and opens the door, natural indoor lighting, realistic home interior, storytelling beginning.
Frame 3: Outside her house, she boards her bicycle, one foot on the pedal, preparing to ride, residential street background, cinematic morning light.
Frame 4: She rides the bicycle on the street toward the gym, side-angle action shot, natural motion blur, dynamic composition, urban environment.
Frame 5: She arrives at the gym exterior and parks her bicycle near the entrance, modern gym building with glass facade, confident expression, realistic reflections.
Frame 6: Inside the gym, she performs an exercise such as lifting dumbbells or doing squats, focused expression, visible effort, professional gym environment, dramatic lighting.
Frame 7: She takes a break and drinks water from a bottle, slightly sweaty, natural post-workout moment, blurred gym equipment background, candid realism.
Frame 8: She exits the gym and walks toward her bicycle, carrying her gym bag, relaxed and satisfied expression, warm evening light, cinematic atmosphere.
Frame 9: She rides her bicycle back toward home during golden hour sunset, peaceful and relaxed mood, cinematic street scene, storytelling conclusion.
```

  "negative_prompt": "stylization, aesthetic correction, body normalization, shape refinement, geometric reinterpretation, anatomical distortion, breast reduction, volume compression, torso slimming, ribcage narrowing, symmetry correction, proportion changes, depth flattening, shadow softening, beautification filters, perspective alteration"

## High-Detail Tanned Woman Portrait in Bikini Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
High angle, slightly top-down medium close-up portrait (waist up) of a young adult Caucasian woman with a slim, fit body and flat stomach. She has deeply tanned, glowing sun-kissed skin, with her face slightly lighter but bronzed and luminous. A distinct pale tan line runs horizontally across her chest above the bikini top.

She has an oval face with a defined jawline and soft chin, light blue/grey eyes with heavy eyelids, long thick dark eyelashes, soft light-blonde eyebrows with a subtle arch, a small straight nose with a gentle highlight, high cheekbones with flushed pink blush and glossy highlight, and full plump glossy soft pink lips slightly parted. Her expression is relaxed, confident, and subtly seductive, looking directly into the camera.

Her hair is long, thick platinum blonde with subtle darker roots, styled in loose romantic beach waves, parted slightly off-center, falling over her right shoulder and tucked behind her left ear.

She is wearing a dark teal strapless bandeau bikini top with gathered fabric and a small metallic O-ring in the center, and matching dark teal high-cut bikini bottoms pulled high on the hips, with her right hand lightly pulling up the bikini string. She wears two delicate layered gold chain necklaces (one with a tiny pearl or bead), a thin silver ring on her right index finger, and a beaded bracelet near her right wrist.

Tattoos: a small fine-line black heart outline with an unreadable word inside on the inner left forearm, the word “bisou” in delicate red cursive below it, and a faint small black script tattoo on the right upper thigh/hip bone.

She is reclining back on slightly wrinkled white bedsheets in a cozy indoor bedroom, leaning against a curved white headboard and a textured cream chunky-knit pillow. Soft, bright natural diffused daylight creates gentle highlights and realistic soft shadows. In the background, a flat white wall with a European-style dual round electrical outlet is visible. Aspect ratio 4:5, photorealistic, highly detailed, 8k resolution.
```

}

An adult 21+ ethnically ambiguous person in their late-20s with a sun-kissed complexion, curly hair, and a fit athletic build is the hero, lounging on the hood of a vintage-looking but unbranded car in a desert rest stop at golden hour, one leg bent and boot planted, chin lifted with fearless swagger, wearing a cropped leather jacket over a ribbed bandeau and high-rise trousers, more skin shown yet fully tasteful, surrounded by non-legible road forms and abstract signage-free silhouettes; a low warm key from the setting sun hits from camera-right with hard-edged glow, fill is a soft bounce from sand to open shadows, negative fill from a black flag deepens the jacket contours, and a crisp rim from sun wrap outlines hair, with highlights carefully rolled to avoid clipping and speculars on leather controlled; shot on a 135mm lens at f/2.4, ISO 100, 1/1000s for strong compression, subject centered with vast negative space sky, foreground heat-haze shimmer and slight occlusion from a side mirror adding depth; skin pores, dust microtexture, and natural tonal gradients visible, curls separated strand by strand, leather grain and stitching tactile, sand and metal patina believable; graded warm with restrained saturation, deep shadows retaining detail, subtle grain, texture-preserving retouch, and in summary a sun-baked roadside couture attitude piece with hard golden key, sculpted contrast, and telephoto cinematic compression.
```

## High-Fashion Editorial Portrait of a Confident Woman

> Lüks moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-fashion editorial portrait of a young woman from the reference image. A confident woman sits in a modern designer chair, wearing a sharp, tailored black power suit with a structured blazer and sleek black silk blouse, no tie, paired with elegant pointed-toe heels.Her posture is relaxed yet commanding, legs crossed with effortless poise, conveying quiet strength and self-assurance.
The setting is a contemporary studio with concrete-gray walls and soft professional softbox lighting subtly visible at the edges of the frame. Behind her hangs a large expressive abstract painting of her own face, rendered in neutral beige, black, and charcoal tones, featuring bold brush strokes and subtle paint drips, and below is written the signature PROMPTX
```

## Vibrant Chaotic Slime Collage

> Görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A close-up, overhead shot showcases a vibrant, chaotic collage of colorful slime, glitter, beads, and small decorative objects. The composition is densely packed with various textures and shapes, dominated by bright rainbow colors. Scattered throughout are small, cube-shaped letter beads spelling out "SLOW DOWN, YOU'RE DOING FINE." Other embellishments include rhinestones, pearls, plastic flowers, stars, hearts, and sprinkled glitter, all embedded within or adhered to the gooey, textured slime. The overall effect is a playful, almost overwhelming, explosion of color and sparkle.
```

Above the child — an older child, positioned right behind them, both hands gently resting on the crown of the younger child's head, fingers intertwined, soft lively smile. Above them, a woman — both hands gently resting on the crown of the older child's head, fingers intertwined, soft lively smile. At the top — a man, standing behind the woman, both palms folded and gently resting on her head, fingers intertwined, looking directly at the camera, calm and confident facial expression. Everyone is wearing identical black turtlenecks without decorations. Background — completely black, without texture. ## Couture Woman and Colossal Dragon in Renaissance Palace

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an 8K UHD, hyper-realistic editorial fashion portrait using the attached photo as the face reference and identity lock.
A young woman walks in profile from left to right, positioned beneath the massive head of a dragon. Her movement is elegant, slow, and confident, like a luxury runway moment captured mid-stride.
She wears a couture emerald velvet strapless gown with structured bodice and dramatic silhouette. The skirt features intricate gold baroque embroidery, botanical motifs, and vintage tapestry textures. A long, opulent train flows behind her, made of layered silk, organza, and metallic jacquard, embedded with micro-crystals, antique beads, and delicate lace filigree, catching the light like liquid gold.
She wears pointed metallic heels with engraved detailing, oversized statement earrings, and carries a refined Gucci handbag with monogram canvas and gold hardware. Hair slicked back in a clean high-fashion style, sharp cheekbones, editorial makeup, confident expression.
There are two main subjects: a colossal ornamental dragon and the couture woman.
The dragon is depicted in an elegant, regal, fashion-heraldic style rather than monstrous. Its scales resemble layered luxury textiles — embossed leather, velvet, silk brocade, and quilted patterns. Colors: deep emerald, antique gold, and black.
Gold chains, tassels, pearl strands, and baroque charms hang between its scales. Subtle interlocking Gucci monogram patterns appear woven into the wings and tail like haute couture fabric.
Its horns curve gracefully like gilded sculpture. The dragon stands tall in profile, towering and protective, dwarfing the woman, exuding majesty and luxury rather than aggression.
Background Prompt:
An immense Renaissance-inspired marble palace interior with grand staircases, carved arches, and towering columns. Polished floors reflect soft golden light. Floating dust particles, drifting silk ribbons, and faint floral shadows in the air. Marble veined with gold and green tones. Atmosphere serene, aristocratic, timeless, ultra-luxurious couture editorial aesthetic. Monochrome emerald-gold palette with cinematic depth.
Lighting & Camera:
Soft diffused luxury fashion lighting, gentle rim light on fabric textures, high dynamic range.
Shot on Canon EOS R5, 85mm f/1.2L, 8K ultra-HD, razor-sharp detail, shallow depth of field, Vogue editorial photography, hyper-real textures, cinematic luxury campaign.
```

Oval face with defined cheekbones, soft jawline, slightly rounded chin. Light blue-green eyes, almond-shaped, direct confident gaze slightly downward into camera with calm neutral-to-serious expression. Small straight refined nose with soft tip. Full naturally pigmented lips, rosy-pink nude color, lips together in a relaxed closed neutral expression, not smiling. Softly arched natural medium brown eyebrows, slightly bold and defined. Minimal natural makeup — light coverage foundation, subtle brow definition, rosy lip color, light mascara, no heavy contouring, natural sun-flushed cheeks with a warm glow. Long platinum white-blonde hair, voluminous, loose beachy waves, slightly tousled, falling past chest and shoulders, parted slightly off-center.

Wearing a white ribbed sleeveless crop tank top, scoop neckline, fitted, cropped above the navel exposing midriff. Dark charcoal grey low-rise slim straight-leg jeans with silver button detail at waistband, slightly faded wash. Carrying a brown distressed suede leather shoulder bag with gold studded detail and buckle hardware, strap crossing over left shoulder, bag hanging at hip level on back side. Silver and crystal beaded bracelets stacked on left wrist, small silver ring on left hand finger. White manicured nails.

Standing leaning casually against a wall corner, left shoulder and back resting against the wall, body slightly angled, right hand resting on right hip, weight shifted to one side, relaxed confident pose. Face turned slightly toward camera.

Background is a sharp corner where two wall surfaces meet — left side is white painted brick wall texture, right side is slate blue-grey horizontal wood panel siding or garage door. Ground is grey asphalt pavement visible at bottom. No people or other objects in background. Setting appears to be an outdoor urban or suburban exterior on a sunny day.

Bright warm golden late-afternoon sunlight hitting subject from front-left, casting warm golden glow on skin, face and hair illuminated, creating soft shadows on right side of face and body. High resolution sharp image, medium full-body shot from approximately knee level upward, slightly low angle looking up, 3:4 vertical portrait orientation, natural warm golden-hour color grading, casual street style lifestyle aesthetic.
```

A cinematic high-fashion harvest portrait of myself, keeping my exact face unchanged. IMPORTANT: Do not alter the face of the reference face; the output must have 100% exact facial features as the reference image. I am draped in a voluminous, asymmetrical dark red floral silk organza gown, cinched at the waist with a raw, distressed black leather belt for an edgy editorial contrast. My hair is styled in a messy, voluminous textured updo with loose, wind-swept tendrils framing my face. The makeup is minimalist-flawless matte skin, sharp mascara, and softly flushed cheeks. The scene takes place in an open, sprawling strawberry field under a dramatic, moody overcast sky with a thick atmospheric fog rolling in. Hard rim lighting from an off-camera strobe cuts through the gloom, highlighting my silhouette and the translucent silk of my dress. Shot with an 85mm lens for flattering compression, featuring a stylized 35mm film aesthetic with subtle, nostalgic grain. Ensure the output maintains 100% exact facial features from the reference image; do not alter the face. -ar 2:3 Written vertically in the "lower-right corner" of the image is a clear, visible watermark that reads "Shreya".

The table is adorned with a clear glass vase containing fresh pink roses, a small white ceramic vase, and a wooden bowl filled with citrus fruit. Sunlight streams intensely from large windows on the left, casting dramatic, warm shadows across the polished walnut hardwood floors. A classic white architectural archway separates the dining area from the background living room. Through the arch, a beige sofa, a coffee table, and a large potted indoor tree are visible, with French doors revealing a glimpse of a lush green garden outside. A brass chandelier with small shades hangs above the dining table. The lighting is golden and atmospheric, evoking a sense of peaceful luxury and timeless elegance. 8k resolution, architectural photography style.

## Subject & Character
Main Subject: Interior room scene
Subject Type: Architecture / Interior Design

Direction: Side lighting from left windows
Quality: Soft directional sunlight creating distinct shadows on the floor
Temperature: Warm golden tones (approx 3500K)

Highlights: Sheen on the painted table, reflections on hardwood floor, glass vase highlights
Time of Day: Golden hour / Late afternoon
Lighting Setup: Natural fill with practical fixtures

Color Scheme: Warm neutrals
Temperature: Warm
Contrast: Medium-High due to bright sunlight and interior shadows
Saturation: Natural, slightly warm and vibrant
Color Grading: Warm, cozy, slightly vintage interior magazine look
Accent Colors: Pink roses, orange citrus fruit, fresh green leaves

## Composition & Camera
Camera Angle: Eye-level
Shot Type: Wide interior shot
Focal Length: 24mm to 35mm wide angle
Depth of Field: Deep depth of field, mostly sharp throughout
Perspective: One-point perspective
Rule of Thirds: Table anchors lower left, archway frames the right two-thirds

## Cozy Split-Level Studio Apartment with Rainy Garden View

```
A highly detailed, photorealistic vertical wide shot of a cozy, modern split-level studio apartment. The composition captures the full height of the room, featuring a loft bedroom on the upper level with a bed, white linens, and a sleek metal railing. A light wooden staircase ascends on the right side. Below the loft is a compact kitchenette with white cabinets. The main living area in the foreground features a comfortable white L-shaped sectional sofa, a round wooden coffee table with a cup, and a television on a low stand playing a colorful cartoon. The defining feature is the large glass patio doors at the back, revealing a lush garden scene with heavy rain falling, creating visible streaks on the glass and a moody, cool atmosphere outside that contrasts with the warm, inviting tungsten lighting inside. The floor is light oak wood. Decor includes potted plants, wall sconces, and framed art. The image quality is 8K, sharp, and resembles high-end architectural photography.
```

## Three-Panel Transformation Journey Artwork Prompt

> Lüks illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a three-panel grid artwork showing a beautiful young woman portrayed as a symbolic journey of transformation. Each panel must feature a different pose, setting, and emotional tone while clearly showing it is the same person.
Left Panel — “Stillness / Origins”

A beautiful woman with short dark hair, luminous skin, and striking eyes sits on a large stone beside a calm lake at sunrise.

She is shown in a relaxed seated pose, slightly turned away from the viewer, looking toward the horizon.

She wears a soft flowing pastel dress with elegant folds.

The background features misty water, distant hills, and warm early sunlight.

The mood should feel quiet, reflective, and poetic — symbolizing beginnings and inner calm.
Center Panel — “Presence / Becoming”

The same woman now stands confidently in a modern city rooftop garden at golden hour.

She faces forward with a powerful but natural stance, one hand lightly touching her hair as it moves in the wind.

Her outfit becomes more refined: a stylish flowing dress with subtle contemporary elegance.

Background includes skyline silhouettes, soft sunlight, and greenery, symbolizing growth, awareness, and stepping into life.
Right Panel — “Freedom / Expression”

The same woman is shown in motion on a dramatic coastal cliff with the ocean behind her.

She is captured mid-spin or mid-stride, hair and dress flowing strongly in the wind.

Her pose should feel energetic, open, and fearless.

Background shows crashing waves, wide sky, and dynamic light — symbolizing liberation, expansion, and self-realization.
```

## Chocolate Crepe Commercial Render Prompt (Python Class Style)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
class ChocolateCrepeAd:
    def __init__(self):
        self.resolution = "8K_ULTRA_HD"
        self.frame_ratio = "9:16"  # Optimized for Social Media
        https://t.co/cI7K2nrLBL = "Hyper-Realistic / Professional Styling"

    def setup_scene(self):
        # Background & Environment
        self.surface = cr.Surface(material="Rustic Wooden Board", texture="Grainy")
        self.background = Bokeh(source="Vibrant Green Plants", blur_intensity=0.8)
        https://t.co/ci6mMG4fGy = NaturalLight(time="Warm Golden Hour", mood="Soft Cinematic")

        # The Subject: Gourmet Crepes
        self.subject = cr.FoodItem(
            type="Ultra-Thin Golden Crepes",
            form="Tight Cylinders",
            quantity="Casual Stack",
            attributes={
                "edges": "Lightly Browned / Crispy",
                "topping": "Elegant Dark Chocolate Spiral Stripes",
                "filling": "Rich Glossy Chocolate-Hazelnut Spread"
            }
        )

    def apply_motion_effects(self):
        # Dynamic Elements
        FluidDynamics.add_drizzle(
            substance="Molten Chocolate",
            speed="Slow-Motion",
            behavior="Falling / Coating"
        )
        SteamParticles.emit(source=self.subject, opacity=0.3)
        
        # Camera Movement
        https://t.co/Oq1q7ViC4J = https://t.co/Wl1DQQj2RB(mode="Macro")
        https://t.co/Oq1q7ViC4J.path = ["Slow Pan", "Tilt", "Slight Rotation"]
        https://t.co/Oq1q7ViC4J.focus = "Gooey ends and creamy textures"

    def render_commercial(self):
        return self.setup_scene().apply_motion_effects().execute(fps=60)
```

Frame 2: A side profile with exaggerated posture, emphasizing the contours of the face and neck, eyes piercing forward, bathed in colorful gel lighting blending neon lavender, teal, and magenta highlights for a futuristic, edgy feel.

Shot on a high-end mirrorless camera with ultra-high 8K resolution, cinematic depth of field, authentic skin texture, and editorial-grade post-processing. Keep the exact 4x4 layout, crop, spacing, poses, gestures, hand placements, and framing in every panel. PRIMARY IDENTITY ANCHOR: use the uploaded reference photo of the target adult man. Replace the man in ALL panels with the reference identity while preserving the original pose and expression intensity. Keep suit-and-tie styling and clean studio background. Maintain consistent identity across all 16 panels."
    },
    "output": {
      "aspect_ratio": "1:1",
      "resolution": "ultra_high",

      "grain": "subtle_analog_bw"
    },
    "scene": {
      "environment": "clean white studio backdrop",
      "lighting": {
        "style": "high-key studio",
        "key_light": "front softbox",
        "fill": "balanced fill",
        "avoid": "dramatic cinematic shadows"
      },
      "camera": {
        "lens": "50mm",
        "framing": "mid chest to head (varies per panel but keep same as structure image)",
        "focus": "eyes sharp"
      }
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "adult man",
      "wardrobe": "black suit, white shirt, black tie (match structure image), no logos",
      "grooming": "match reference hairstyle as closely as possible while keeping overall look consistent",
      "anatomy_rules": "realistic hands, correct fingers, no distortions"
    },
    "style": {
      "color": "pure black and white",
      "contrast": "medium_high",
      "background": "keep identical to structure image",
      "finish": "clean studio print look"
    },
    "quality_control": {
      "identity_lock": "strict",
      "hands_priority": "very_high",
      "avoid": [

        "deformed hands",

      "distorted anatomy",

playful wink with a wide smile showing teeth, one hand near mouth
surprised face with wide eyes, mouth open, both hands on cheeks
pouting/annoyed face with brows slightly furrowed, cheeks resting in palms
serious confident look with neutral lips, both arms raised and hands behind head adjusting hair.

## Cosplay Portrait with Flash Photography

```
Realistic vertical 9:16 portrait of a young Caucasian blonde American girl in her early 20s with natural fair-to-light skin tone, soft neutral-to-warm undertones, and a healthy youthful glow with slight flash sheen. She has long, straight, sleek platinum-blonde hair, center-parted, falling over her shoulders. Her face is heart-shaped with a sharp jawline, smooth forehead, almond-shaped light hazel or blue eyes, subtle smokey eye makeup, long lashes, and groomed arched brows in darker blonde/light brown. She has a straight nose with a refined button tip and slight highlighter, high cheekbones with contour and soft glowing highlighter, and full plump nude-pink glossy lips with a neutral, slightly pouty expression and confident direct gaze.
She has a fit, athletic, toned body with visible muscle definition in shoulders and thighs. She wears a Spider-Man inspired tight spandex/lycra bodysuit: sleeveless with a high-neck choker collar, keyhole chest cutout, red upper panel with black web pattern, and blue textured bodice. Detached long blue arm warmers with red trim and white elbow accents. High-cut legs with blue thigh-high stockings featuring a red band and a small white/pink spider emblem on the thigh.
Pose: leaning back against a waist-high rough concrete rooftop wall, arms extended sideways gripping the edge, legs close together with right leg slightly crossed, facing the camera.
Setting: rooftop or high-rise balcony at night with a dark sky and blurred city skyline full of illuminated windows and bokeh lights, urban nightlife atmosphere.
Lighting: direct on-camera flash creating bright subject illumination, strong contrast, and hard shadows on the wall behind.
Camera: eye-level, straight-on.
Style: realism, amateur flash photography, cosplay portrait, influencer aesthetic.
```

## Vogue-Style Beauty Portrait (Dilireba Dilmurat)

> Sinematik, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Beautiful close-up headshot of Dilireba Dilmurat, captured in a refined, Vogue-style beauty portrait. Her porcelain skin has a soft, luminous glow, appearing smooth yet natural with delicate skin texture visible under professional studio lighting. The lighting is carefully balanced to create gentle highlights across her cheekbones and forehead, with soft, cinematic shadows sculpting her features for depth and dimension.

Her big, expressive brown eyes look directly into the camera with quiet confidence and elegance. The gaze is intense yet graceful, enhanced by thick, natural brows that frame her eyes beautifully. Her sharp nose bridge adds definition to her profile, while her bold matte red lips provide a striking contrast against her flawless complexion, creating a timeless high-fashion aesthetic.

Her long, silky dark hair is parted in the middle, falling in soft, loose waves that frame her face delicately. The texture of her hair appears smooth and glossy, with subtle movement that adds realism and softness to the composition. A navy coat collar is visible at the bottom of the frame, layered over a crisp white shirt underneath, introducing a sophisticated contrast of deep navy and bright white tones.

The background is clean and minimalist white, ensuring full focus remains on her face. The professional studio lighting setup produces even illumination with soft shadows, emphasizing facial structure without harshness. Every detail is rendered in high clarity—natural pores, subtle highlights on the lips, fine hair strands, and realistic skin texture—contributing to a highly detailed, photorealistic finish.

Cinematic composition, ultra-high detail, sharp focus, flawless yet natural skin rendering, fashion editorial beauty shot, 8k resolution, aspect ratio 3:4, stylize 150, luxury magazine aesthetic.
```

## Ultra-Realistic Valentine's Day Editorial with Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic fashion photo, vertical 9:16 format; IDENTITY LOCK (CRITICAL): use the face from the attached photo as the only identity reference; preserve 100% the exact same appearance without any changes — face shape, eyes, lips, nose, cheekbones, jawline, hairline, hair color and texture, skin tone, natural asymmetry, and micro-texture; DO NOT alter appearance, no rejuvenation, no beautification, no face swap.

Location: bright minimalist room/interior, white wall background, large red “paint” lettering with drips on the wall reading “Valentine’s Day” (graffiti/dripping style), text placed strictly in the upper part of the frame.

In the frame: a large white soft sofa with voluminous cushions; red rose petals scattered on the sofa and around the model. On the right side of the model, on the sofa, lies a very large bouquet of red roses (dense, multi-headed, with green leaves), occupying the right third of the frame.

Model: adult woman, looking directly into the camera, confident and sensual expression, bold red lipstick, light natural makeup, real skin texture (visible pores, micro-contrast, no retouching, no plastic look).

Hair: long, slightly tousled, one strand falling onto the face, soft side part.

Pose: woman lying/reclining diagonally on the white sofa — body extended along the sofa, torso slightly lifted on one elbow, one hand raised to the hair near the temple, the other hand relaxed on the pillow in front of her, shoulders open.

Clothing: fitted red slip dress with thin straps (red satin slip dress / red bodycon slip dress), smooth fabric with soft folds along the waist and hips.

Composition: model positioned left/center, bouquet on the right, “Valentine’s Day” text large at the top of the wall, rose petals on the sofa and near her legs, medium shot (waist/upper thigh and above), slight perspective, camera at eye level or slightly higher.

Lighting: natural soft daylight from a side window (soft window light) + subtle fill, warm soft shadows, atmospheric editorial look.

Optics/quality: photorealistic, editorial, ultra-sharp, high detail, 35mm look, f/2.8–f/4, ISO 100–200, 1/125, HDR, natural skin texture, subtle film grain.

NEGATIVE: different face/appearance, glossy “plastic” skin, heavy retouching/airbrush, CGI/3D, illustration, extra objects, other text, spelling errors, blurred face, extra fingers/deformations.
```

## 64K DSLR Urban Fashion Photoshoot Collage

> Minimal, lüks logo üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Transform this image [Upload Your Image] into a 64K DSLR shot resolution of a stylish urban fashion photoshoot presented as a 3x3 collage, featuring a young man (same face as the uploaded image) captured in candid lifestyle poses across a modern city setting. He has short straight brown hair, light skin, and wears black rectangular eyeglasses. His outfit is minimal and coordinated: a deep burgundy baseball cap with subtle embroidered text, an oversized white sweatshirt with a small elegant graphic logo on the chest, burgundy tailored trousers with a clean modern fit, and clean white sneakers. He carries a small black crossbody bag in some frames.

Each panel captures a different natural pose adjusting his glasses, walking, leaning against a wall, turning casually, lifting one arm confidently, or holding the brim of his cap, creating a relaxed, confident, everyday fashion mood. His expressions range from thoughtful and calm to cheerful and carefree.

The setting is an outdoor urban environment with modern architecture, cobblestone walkways, neutral-toned buildings, large wooden planters, and leafless trees decorated with red lantern-style lights. One central frame features a classic storefront façade labeled “{argument name="storefront label" default="[Add Text]"},” adding a European café street aesthetic.

Backgrounds are softly blurred, emphasizing the subject while maintaining environmental context. Lighting is natural daylight with soft shadows, overcast tones, and balanced exposure. The color palette is cohesive and elegant, burgundy reds, crisp whites, muted greys, and warm wood tones, creating a refined street-style look.

Textures are realistic: cotton fabric folds, natural fabric movement, leather accessories, and stone pavement. Shot in high resolution, lifestyle fashion photography style, shallow depth of field, editorial composition, authentic street photography feel, clean framing, modern Instagram lookbook aesthetic, minimal color grading, soft contrast, natural motion capture.

Octane render & Unreal Engine.
```
